* DONE [#C] Build the minimal AI session-log gatherer

## Purpose
Deliver the smallest usable session-log gatherer: one command copies configured local and remote agent session directories into one operator-selected directory tree.

## Governing plan
- Minimal implementation plan: `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`
- Minimal design: `doc/DESIGN.md`
- Prior research and expanded issues remain historical context only; they are not first-version requirements.

## First-version boundary
The tool reads a small source configuration and invokes ordinary rsync/SSH pulls. It preserves each source-relative path beneath `<collection-root>/<machine>/<agent>/`, updates new or changed files on rerun, never deletes destination files, and never mutates sources.

No database, index, parser, normalization, conversion, object store, manifest chain, archive management, backup, retention, migration, plugin, deployment, or observability subsystem belongs to this root.

** Acceptance Criteria

- One command reads a small configuration of machine, agent, host, and source-directory rows.
- Local and SSH/rsync sources copy byte-for-byte to `<collection-root>/<machine>/<agent>/<source-relative-path>`.
- Reruns are idempotent, changed files update, unchanged files remain unchanged, and no source is mutated.
- Missing directories, unavailable machines, and transfer failures are reported without preventing independent rows from running.
- Focused synthetic tests cover layout, byte identity, changed-file updates, idempotence, failure continuation, and source non-mutation.
- Minimal usage documentation and a configuration example exist.
- All expanded-scope issues are detached or deferred and do not block this root.

## Purpose
execute the bounded architecture-specific Haskell/Python comparison and select the language used by every production package.

## Inputs
the core operation list, language matrix, and reversal threshold in [DESIGN.md](DESIGN.md#124-haskell-versus-python-decision-matrix) and [section 18](DESIGN.md#18-reversal-thresholds-and-bounded-pre-implementation-experiments).

## Outputs
an evidence record covering binary copy/hash, deterministic manifest encoding, subprocess timeout/cancellation, SQLite FTS/backup access, closure composition, and builds on Darwin arm64, Linux arm64, and Linux x86_64; a recorded Haskell or Python decision.

## Declared prerequisites
`none`.

## Traceability
`R13`, `R14`, `D12`, `D13`, `D18`, `E05`, `Q13`, `RC07`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `GATE-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
the spike has no archive path, private fixture, source-host access, or deployment output. Failure changes only the language decision; object, manifest, adapter, and recovery contracts remain unchanged.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
both bounded implementations are compared on closure reproducibility, unsafe FFI, adapter boundary size, failure behavior, and maintainability; Haskell remains selected unless the documented core-risk threshold is met; the spike remains outside the production tree.

## Required tests or review evidence
build and run the bounded operations against synthetic bytes and SQLite data on all three systems; record exact commands, closure/tool versions, failures, and comparative code boundaries.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
determine which filesystem metadata belongs to native custody and how parser fixtures can preserve structure without exposing private content.

## Inputs
manifest metadata contract, macOS uncertainty `Q06`, fixture uncertainty `Q15`, and privacy constraint `RC10`.

## Outputs
per-platform metadata allowlists for modes, symlinks, xattrs, resource forks, and hardlink groups; a synthetic/structural-redaction fixture policy with review and provenance rules.

## Declared prerequisites
`none`.

## Traceability
`R1`, `R8`, `R11`, `R15`, `D6`, `D14`, `D17`, `Q06`, `Q15`, `RC10`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `GATE-11`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
the default is conservative exclusion with a recorded reason. Policy expansion is versioned and does not rewrite prior manifests.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
admitted metadata can be captured, hashed or referenced, restored, and tested; credential-bearing metadata and real content are excluded; structural redaction cannot be mistaken for native authority.

## Required tests or review evidence
create synthetic files with links, non-UTF-8 path bytes, modes, xattrs, resource forks where supported, malformed records, and secret-shaped canaries; verify capture/restoration and leakage scans.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
select the read-only method that distinguishes an exact coherent DB/WAL/SHM bundle from a consistent logical SQLite snapshot.

## Inputs
SQLite artifact distinctions and the live-store hard gate in `D8`, `D18`, `Q05`, and `RC03`.

## Outputs
a per-filesystem method matrix for APFS/filesystem snapshots, coordinated read helpers, Online Backup, `VACUUM INTO`, and `sqlite3_rsync`, with exact-versus-logical classification and disruption constraints.

## Declared prerequisites
`none`.

## Traceability
`R1`, `R5`, `R8`, `R11`, `D8`, `D10`, `D18`, `E03`, `Q05`, `S05`, `RC03`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `GATE-02`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
trials use disposable databases only and prohibit checkpoint, vacuum, migration, or repair of a producer store. If no safe exact method exists, retain the architecture’s incomplete-state behavior and return the affected adapter to design review.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
concurrent-write trials establish which method, if any, proves a coherent exact bundle on each target filesystem; every unproved result is classified `incoherent` or `unknown`, never `complete`.

## Required tests or review evidence
generate synthetic WAL workloads with commits before, during, and after capture; compare SQLite integrity, transaction visibility, source artifact hashes/metadata, and reader behavior.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

    "text": "GATE-02 failure/root-cause checkpoint\n\nFailure signature count: 1 (corrected; reset pending three-system pass).\n\nThe first pinned aarch64-darwin check failed only at the sqlite3_rsync source-health predicate. The replica was integrity-valid and transactionally consistent, but after sqlite3_rsync returned the producer main file had been rewritten, `-wal`/`-shm` had disappeared, and a fresh reader did not see the still-open producer connection's post-capture commit.\n\nThis was reproduced three ways against SQLite 3.53.3 / source ID d4c0e51e4aeb96955b99185ab9cde75c339e2c29c3f3f12428d364a10d782c62: idle producer, active read transaction, and `--wal-only`. All returned 0, changed the source artifact set, removed WAL/SHM, and left the next producer commit invisible through the pathname. Pinned source inspection explains the behavior: `tool/sqlite3_rsync.c` opens ORIGIN with `SQLITE_OPEN_READWRITE`, begins a transaction, and closes normally without `SQLITE_DBCONFIG_NO_CKPT_ON_CLOSE` or persistent-WAL protection. SQLite's close behavior can therefore checkpoint/materialize the source and remove sidecars.\n\nCorrected gate contract (not a weakened assertion): sqlite3_rsync remains classified `logical_consistent` because its replica passes integrity and visibility checks, but is `rejected_source_mutation` for this project's non-mutating producer boundary. Online Backup and VACUUM INTO remain eligible logical methods only if the producer path remains healthy through the after-capture commit. Producer-coordinated quiescence is the sole exact `complete` physical row; uncoordinated interleaved physical capture remains `incoherent` even when readable.\n\nArtifacts: `~/Products/sessions/gate-02/` (`aarch64-darwin-build-failure-1.log`, immutable failed result path `/nix/store/wbawh9ly3kxz1g62z3avmsgd4p7v0sb3-gate02-concurrent-wal-aarch64-darwin/result.json`, three `sqlite3-rsync-*-probe.json` files, pinned source and local corrected result).\n\nExact resume step: build the corrected concurrent-WAL check on aarch64-darwin, aarch64-linux, and x86_64-linux; inspect observed sandbox filesystems and the five method rows; then run the bounded NFSv4 negative trial and produce the per-filesystem decision matrix. ## Purpose
retain or reverse `hera` as the sole hub by proving adequate capacity, administrative control, local throughput, headroom, and fail-safe low-space behavior.

## Inputs
aggregate fleet evidence, the provisional topology, capacity reversal threshold, `Q07`, `Q08`, and the operator-restored-directory boundary.

## Outputs
an approved privacy-safe sizing method; authority/staging/rebuild headroom calculation; measured synthetic or separately authorized metadata-only write/hash/verify throughput; administrative-control assessment; selected hub role; hard free-space floor; and a content-free low-space test receipt.

## Declared prerequisites
`none`.

## Traceability
`R8`, `R10`, `R12`, `R15`, `D7`, `D14`, `D15`, `D16`, `D18`, `E01`, `Q07`, `Q08`, `S10`, `RC08`, `RC10`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `GATE-03`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
no private-byte measurement, backup endpoint access, repository creation, credential use, source-host access, or host mutation occurs. Synthetic data remains in an isolated local temporary namespace and is removed exactly. If `hera` fails the threshold, keep hub-dependent packages blocked and return the hub role to architecture review.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
the chosen hub is under the operator’s administrative control; available capacity is at least 2× projected 12-month authority plus simultaneous staging and rebuild headroom under the approved conservative assumptions; synthetic write/hash/verify measurements support the intended local workload; and crossing the hard floor stops new acquisition before authority or the filesystem is endangered while verification and search remain available. The decision makes no claim about any external backup.

## Required tests or review evidence
measure local filesystem write, hash, verification, and cleanup throughput with bounded synthetic data; model the largest observed source generation, staging, and index rebuild overlap; test startup below the hard floor, crossing the floor during staged acquisition, reservation races, and recovery after space is returned; and verify that no endpoint, repository, or credential is accessed.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
retain scheduled SSH/rsync pull reconciliation as the completeness mechanism under outage, interruption, and retention pressure.

## Inputs
one-writer topology, source lifecycle evidence, and experiment `E02`.

## Outputs
a transport/cadence decision record covering pinned-host SSH, partial rsync state, timeout, interruption, resume, offline age, and catch-up on all three system architectures.

## Declared prerequisites
`none`.

## Traceability
`R3`, `R4`, `R7`, `R12`, `D7`, `D9`, `D18`, `E02`, `S02`, `S03`, `RC02`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `GATE-04`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
use local synthetic SSH fixtures or isolated test machines approved for the experiment. A failure may revise transport or cadence, but it cannot introduce a second writer or make hooks authoritative.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
synthetic sources converge after repeated interruption and offline intervals without bidirectional merge, source mutation, duplicate authority, or reliance on lifecycle hooks; any cadence requirement not established by evidence remains a user gate.

## Required tests or review evidence
simulate unreachable hosts, changed files during transfer, stale partials, source truncation, and catch-up; assert that other hosts proceed and prior complete generations remain searchable.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

* DONE [#C] GATE-05 — Validate SQLite/FTS fitness    :gate:
:PROPERTIES:

:EXTERNAL_REF: implementation-plan:GATE-05

## Purpose
confirm that the disposable SQLite/FTS5 tier meets measured lexical search, index-size, and rebuild needs before implementing it as baseline.

## Inputs
query/coverage contract, observed structural scale, and `E04`; no invented latency or rebuild target.

## Outputs
a reproducible synthetic benchmark corpus and results for index build, update, query facets, coverage accounting, index size, and full rebuild; comparison notes for AgentsView, `cass`, and optional DuckDB where lawful and useful.

## Declared prerequisites
`none`.

## Traceability
`R2`, `R9`, `R11`, `D11`, `D18`, `E04`, `Q09`, `S09`, `RC06`, `RC08`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `GATE-05`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
benchmarks contain no real transcript text or private paths. Failure changes only the derived search implementation; authority and recovery contracts remain fixed.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
measured results are presented against user-approved targets, or the target decision remains open under `GATE-08`; any replacement remains disposable and source-linked.

## Required tests or review evidence
run cold rebuild, incremental update, representative lexical/facet queries, malformed/opaque coverage, and index deletion/recreation at no less than the design’s structure-preserving scale.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
determine which existing readers reduce custom parser or UI work without assuming archive custody.

## Inputs
research assessments of AgentsView, Agent Sessions, `cass`, claude-vault, ccusage, ATIF, and Letta; licensing constraint `RC09`.

## Outputs
a source-by-source coverage and behavior matrix, license decision, source-locator assessment, raw-tree requirements, parser diagnostics comparison, and explicit reuse/build decisions.

## Declared prerequisites
`none`.

## Traceability
`R2`, `R9`, `R11`, `D8`, `D11`, `D12`, `D18`, `E08`, `Q14`, `RC06`, `RC09`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `GATE-06`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
candidates execute in isolated derived directories without network or authority credentials. Rejected tools leave no runtime dependency or canonical schema.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
each selected tool operates read-only over synthetic materialized views or derived data, cannot write authority, exposes acceptable provenance/coverage, and has acceptable licensing; unsupported formats remain assigned to the smallest custom reader packages.

## Required tests or review evidence
run candidate tools against synthetic complete, malformed, branched, duplicated, and unknown-version fixtures; compare their outputs to the reader contract rather than to one another.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
turn the design threat model into an accepted custody posture before manifest signing or deployment choices become code.

## Inputs
owner-only plaintext decision, SSH transport protection, SHA-256 chain limits, signature experiment `E06`, operator-managed backup boundary, and unresolved key-custody question `Q11`.

## Outputs
a threat model decision covering hub administrator, compromised source, authority rewrite, offline signing, independently administered immutable anchoring, full-disk encryption, key loss, and recovery.

## Declared prerequisites
`none`.

## Traceability
`R1`, `R8`, `R10`, `R15`, `D14`, `D16`, `D18`, `E06`, `Q11`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `GATE-07`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
no production key is created during evaluation. Signing is not enabled until restore and rotation work without making historical authority unverifiable.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
the baseline unsigned-chain posture is explicitly accepted or a recoverable signing/key-rotation design is selected; source-access credential and key locations are outside Git/Nix; every control has a recovery consequence; no external backup property is inferred.

## Required tests or review evidence
exercise synthetic sign/verify, corrupted chain, wrong key, lost key, rotated key, and hostile path as applicable to the selected posture.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       sess-ek0.1.9
:LABELS:   ["decision-gate","expanded-scope","gate","implementation-plan","phase-0","session-archive","work-package"]

## Purpose
preserve indefinite authority retention by default and define a destructive policy only when capacity, legal, or privacy requirements demand it.

## Inputs
`GATE-03`, `GATE-07`, retention design, experiment `E09`, and `Q12`.

## Outputs
an accepted baseline policy with authority GC disabled; if triggered, a separate local hold/redaction/deletion design with dry-run, confirmation, audit, recovery consequences, and an explicit statement that operator-managed copies are outside the tool’s control.

## Declared prerequisites
`GATE-03`, `GATE-07`.

## Traceability
`R1`, `R2`, `R10`, `R15`, `D16`, `D18`, `E09`, `Q12`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `GATE-09`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
absence of an approved exceptional policy means no authority deletion command or module option exists. A failed destructive-policy test leaves GC disabled.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
all non-authoritative retention is distinguished from authority deletion; any local erasure path has explicit user/legal authorization and proof over the local authority; the tool does not claim deletion from an external copy.

## Required tests or review evidence
with synthetic manifests, prove that ordinary cleanup affects only staging/derived generations; if destructive policy is selected, prove local hold reachability, dry-run accuracy, tombstone/audit behavior, and fail-closed handling of unknown external-copy disposition.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

** Design

validation is pure and runs before filesystem mutation. A new config version is additive; prior configuration remains readable or fails with an explicit migration requirement.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

invalid UUIDs, role collisions, duplicate source scopes, unsafe/world-readable paths, secret values in configuration, unknown required versions, and conflicting writers are rejected before side effects; display labels remain distinct from stable IDs.

## Required tests or review evidence
table/property tests for parsing, deterministic rendering where required, ID namespace separation, rename/rebuild migration, secret-shaped values, path ownership/mode validation, and forward-compatible optional fields.

## Purpose
centralize path containment, hostile-name handling, safe streaming, atomic file operations, subprocess execution, timeout, cancellation, and content-free error classification.

## Inputs
`FND-01`, `FND-02`, threat model `D14`, and metadata representation contract `D6`.

## Outputs
reviewed primitives for open-beneath-root traversal, no-follow enumeration, reversible path-byte encoding, file-kind rejection, atomic temporary-file publication, fsync discipline, argument-vector subprocesses, environment allowlists, controlled streams, timeout/cancellation, and secret-safe diagnostics.

## Declared prerequisites
`FND-01`, `FND-02`.

## Traceability
`R1`, `R8`, `R11`, `R15`, `D5`, `D6`, `D12`, `D14`, `Q06`, `Q15`, `RC10`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `FND-03`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
primitives default closed at trust boundaries and write only unique staging files before atomic publication. A primitive failure returns a typed content-free error and leaves the prior published state intact.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
callers cannot escape configured roots, follow an unapproved symlink, invoke through shell interpolation, log payload bytes, inherit undeclared environment variables, or leave a partially published file after process failure.

## Required tests or review evidence
property/fuzz tests for `..`, absolute paths, NUL, non-UTF-8 bytes, symlink races, devices/sockets, malicious filenames, terminal/HTML control text, large streams, timeout, cancellation, broken pipes, secret canaries, fsync/rename failure, and process exit classes.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
insert and verify immutable exact-byte objects in the SHA-256 namespace.

## Inputs
`FND-02`, `FND-03`, object hierarchy and invariants in `D4`–`D6`.

## Outputs
streaming SHA-256/length computation, temporary-object fsync, atomic insertion, existing-object verification, collision error, object read/verify API, and insertion receipt.

## Declared prerequisites
`FND-02`, `FND-03`.

## Traceability
`R1`, `R4`, `R6`, `D4`, `D5`, `D6`, `D9`, `S03`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `AUTH-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
objects are immutable and inserted only by atomic rename from the authority filesystem. Unreferenced valid objects are harmless; no automatic object deletion is implemented.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
inserted bytes hash to their object ID; duplicate bytes reuse storage only after verification; a same-name/different-byte or length mismatch is fatal; crashes cannot publish partial objects.

## Required tests or review evidence
property tests over arbitrary bytes; empty/large/duplicate objects; concurrent identical insertion; injected short write, fsync, rename, disk-full, and collision failures; post-crash namespace scan.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
construct, validate, serialize, chain, and atomically commit source-generation manifests over verified object references.

## Inputs
`FND-02`, `FND-03`, `AUTH-01`, accepted `GATE-07` and manifest contract `D6`.

## Outputs
versioned manifest model; deterministic encoding; raw/display path, metadata, exclusions, bundle/sidecar, stability, warning, fingerprint, state, predecessor, and tree-digest fields; manifest ID calculation; chain validation; atomic commit.

## Declared prerequisites
`AUTH-01`, `FND-02`, `FND-03`, `GATE-07`.

## Traceability
`R1`, `R3`, `R4`, `R5`, `R6`, `R7`, `R11`, `D4`, `D6`, `D9`, `D10`, `D17`, `S03`, `S06`, `GATE-07`.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
a candidate remains outside `manifests/` until all references and policy checks pass. Failure leaves the prior chain head unchanged; old schemas remain immutable.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
byte-identical logical inputs produce identical manifest bytes/ID; path ordering is raw-byte deterministic; every object is verified before commit; predecessor and allowed state transitions validate; committed manifests never change.

## Required tests or review evidence
golden/property tests for deterministic encoding and field preservation; duplicate logical occurrences; unknown fields; all completeness states; missing object; wrong predecessor; chain fork; truncation/deletion successor; clock anomaly; optional signature posture; crash before/after rename.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
verify object/manifest authority and reconstruct source heads without trusting mutable state or derived data.

## Inputs
`AUTH-01`, `AUTH-02`, `FND-03`, authority invariants `D4`, and recovery contract `D16`.

## Outputs
full and incremental chain/object verifier; source-head reconstruction; verification receipt and content-free failure classes.

## Declared prerequisites
`AUTH-01`, `AUTH-02`, `FND-03`.

## Traceability
`R1`, `R6`, `R10`, `D4`, `D9`, `D16`, `S03`, `S09`, `S10`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `AUTH-03`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
verification is read-only. A failure prevents downstream publication or healthy status and leaves authority unchanged.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
verification detects missing, altered, colliding, orphan-reference, and chain-break conditions; absent or corrupt mutable heads reconstruct from valid manifests; no derived artifact participates in authority repair.

## Required tests or review evidence
corrupt/missing object, malformed/forked chain, absent/incorrect mutable heads, wrong schema/hash namespace, injected read failure, and complete reconstruction from manifests alone.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
hold locks, leases, staging, transfer state, run receipts, mutable heads, retries, and watermarks without making any mutable record authoritative.

## Inputs
`FND-02`, `FND-03`, `AUTH-02`, update transaction `D9`, and content-free status contract `D15`.

## Outputs
coordinator-state store; single-writer lock/lease; unique run/source staging; resumable transfer metadata; manifest-derived head reconstruction; retry queue; content-free run receipts; bounded staging cleanup.

## Declared prerequisites
`FND-02`, `FND-03`, `AUTH-02`.

## Traceability
`R2`, `R4`, `R10`, `R12`, `D4`, `D5`, `D9`, `D15`, `S03`, `S09`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `STATE-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
mutable heads update only after manifest commitment. Cleanup proves no committed reference before deletion and never touches `native/` or `manifests/`.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
deleting the entire state store and staging permits reconstruction from manifests and the next reconciliation; stale locks are resolved by lease/process evidence rather than blind deletion; unchanged/unavailable repeats do not accumulate unbounded transitions.

## Required tests or review evidence
concurrent lock acquisition, stale/live owner distinction, crash at each state transition, state deletion/rebuild, partial transfer resume, repeated offline state, staging cleanup with referenced/unreferenced objects, and secret/path leakage scan.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       sess-ek0.3.1

:CREATED_BY: pi
:DEFERRED: [2126-01-01 Tue 09:00]
:EXTERNAL_REF: implementation-plan:CAP-01
:SOURCE_REPO: sessions
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/sessions
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

## Purpose
provide the format-independent probe, plan, acquire, validate, classify, and fingerprint boundary used by every source adapter.

## Inputs
`FND-02`, `FND-03`, adapter/snapshot contract `D8`, anomaly policy `D10`, and fixture policy `GATE-11`.

## Outputs
versioned adapter interface; capability and source-state model; allowlist/exclusion/sidecar plan; staged-entry stream with raw path bytes and stability evidence; coherence token; completeness classification; structural fingerprint; content-free diagnostics.

## Declared prerequisites
`FND-02`, `FND-03`, `GATE-11`.

## Traceability
`R3`, `R5`, `R7`, `R8`, `R11`, `D8`, `D10`, `D17`, `S08`, `RC01`, `RC05`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `CAP-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
acquisition targets a unique staging namespace only. An adapter failure yields a typed source state and cannot insert objects, commit manifests, mutate a producer, or erase prior evidence.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
an adapter can preserve an unknown opaque tree without parsing message content; executable absence does not suppress store discovery; unsupported fingerprints and unknown coherence remain explicit; adapters cannot write authority or index state.

## Required tests or review evidence
contract tests for absent, empty, unavailable, not-applicable, immutable, changing, partial, opaque, malformed, unknown-version, excluded, and missing-sidecar sources; capability negotiation and diagnostic redaction.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
discover and preserve Droid workspace JSONL, including historical stores where the executable is absent.

## Inputs
`CAP-01`, `CAP-02`, `CAP-03`, Droid findings, and `GATE-11`.

## Outputs
session-root/workspace discovery, version-2/unknown fingerprint, sidecar and source-state plan, resume/fork preservation, local/cloud-sync distinction, and SessionEnd hint handling.

## Declared prerequisites
`CAP-01`, `CAP-02`, `CAP-03`, `GATE-11`.

## Traceability
`R3`, `R5`, `R6`, `R7`, `R11`, `D8`, `D10`, `S04`, `S07`, `RC02`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `SRC-05`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
no Droid reindex, cloud sync, API request, or hook mutation occurs during collection; API exports remain separate optional source occurrences.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
historical-only, empty, active, and absent states remain visible; local JSONL is preserved independently of cloud sync/API eligibility; private schema changes produce diagnostics, not loss.

## Required tests or review evidence
synthetic session_start/message/compaction/todo records, unknown types, resume/fork, executable absent with history, no sessions root, changing file, cloud-sync metadata without local content, and hook failure.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
preserve only documented or approved Claude Desktop session roots and attachments while exposing standard-account and cloud completeness gaps.

## Inputs
`CAP-01`, `CAP-02`, `CAP-03`, `CAP-04`, accepted `GATE-10` and `GATE-11`, and Claude Desktop findings.

## Outputs
allowlisted local-agent/Claude-Code session roots, transcript/work/upload/output/memory/audit relationships as approved, exact/logical SQLite handling where discovered, account-export source class, exclusions, and cloud/side-chat unknown states.

## Declared prerequisites
`CAP-01`, `CAP-02`, `CAP-03`, `CAP-04`, `GATE-10`, `GATE-11`.

## Traceability
`R3`, `R5`, `R8`, `R11`, `D8`, `D14`, `Q01`, `S05`, `RC05`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `SRC-06`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
ambiguous roots are quarantined for policy review. No account export, cloud request, login, or desktop mutation occurs without separate authorization.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
approved local session material preserves relative relationships and exact bytes; renderer caches, credentials, cookies, and keychains remain excluded; cloud-only or undocumented surfaces appear as coverage gaps rather than false absence.

## Required tests or review evidence
synthetic third-party-mode layout, missing attachments, audit chain, live database, cloud-only marker, deleted session, unknown standard-account root, cache/credential canaries, and account-export provenance.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
represent Codex Desktop provenance without promoting generic application-profile caches or duplicating shared CLI authority.

## Inputs
`CAP-01`, `CAP-02`, `CAP-03`, `CAP-04`, accepted `GATE-10` and `GATE-11`, and Codex Desktop findings.

## Outputs
shared-rollout provenance rules, approved distinct-root/export source class if evidence emerges, generic-cache exclusions, installed/desktop-only/cloud-only/unknown states, and exact-byte occurrence deduplication rules.

## Declared prerequisites
`CAP-01`, `CAP-02`, `CAP-03`, `CAP-04`, `GATE-10`, `GATE-11`, `SRC-02`.

`R1`, `R3`, `R6`, `R8`, `R11`, `D8`, `D10`, `Q02`, `S05`, `RC05`.

`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `SRC-07`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
the default admits no generic profile root. New vendor documentation or structural evidence changes a versioned allowlist, never prior manifests.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
shared CLI bytes are referenced once physically but retain desktop/source provenance where established; unproved desktop-only content remains `unknown`; browser profile presence alone never becomes a transcript capture.

## Required tests or review evidence
synthetic shared rollout, identical/different occurrence, generic Chromium cache, approved export, desktop-only marker without bytes, live state database, and unknown app version.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose

## Inputs
`CAP-01`, `CAP-02`, `CAP-03`, `CAP-04`, accepted `GATE-10` and `GATE-11`, `SRC-05`, and Factory findings.

## Outputs
shared-local provenance, approved distinct-root/API-export source class, Electron cache/credential exclusions, organization/API eligibility state, and desktop/cloud completeness diagnostics.

`CAP-01`, `CAP-02`, `CAP-03`, `CAP-04`, `GATE-10`, `GATE-11`, `SRC-05`.

## Traceability
`R1`, `R3`, `R6`, `R8`, `R11`, `D8`, `D14`, `Q04`, `S05`, `RC05`.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
no API call, cloud sync, cookie capture, or desktop mutation occurs during baseline collection. Ambiguous data is quarantined.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

## Purpose
supply authenticated pull transport and an allowlisted, non-resident source-helper mode without embedding an SSH implementation.

## Inputs
`CAP-01`, `FND-03`, accepted `GATE-04`, host/source enrollment from `FND-02`, and one-writer topology `D7`.

## Outputs
pinned-host SSH invocation; `BatchMode`; fixed helper operations keyed by configured source ID; bounded environment and path handling; resumable run/source rsync staging; timeout, cancellation, clock/reachability observation, and content-free error mapping.

## Declared prerequisites
`CAP-01`, `FND-02`, `FND-03`, `GATE-04`.

## Traceability
`R4`, `R8`, `R12`, `D7`, `D8`, `D9`, `D12`, `D14`, `E02`, `S02`, `S03`, `RC02`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `CAP-02`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
source operations are read-only and fixed. No general remote shell, producer mutation, push path, peer merge, or transcript-bearing hook is introduced; failed transfers remain unpublished staging.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
all transcript-bearing flow points toward the hub; helper commands cannot select arbitrary roots or outputs; interruption resumes only within the matching run/source namespace; an offline host does not block another source.

## Required tests or review evidence
synthetic SSH server or isolated approved hosts; wrong/pinned host key, authentication failure, timeout, helper path escape, malicious source ID, partial transfer, disconnect, retry, clock skew, large stream, and concurrent-source limits.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

* DEFER [#C] CAP-03 — Implement immutable-file, changing-JSONL, and mutable-tree capture    :cap:
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       sess-ek0.3.3
:LABELS:   ["cap","expanded-scope","implementation-plan","phase-2","session-archive","work-package"]
:ISSUE_TYPE: task
:CREATED:  [2026-08-12 Wed 15:04]
:MODIFIED: [2026-08-13 Thu 13:45]
:CREATED_BY: pi
:DEFERRED: [2126-01-01 Tue 09:00]
:EXTERNAL_REF: implementation-plan:CAP-03
:SOURCE_REPO: sessions
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/sessions
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

## Purpose
acquire ordinary files and directories while distinguishing stable, open, unstable, and partial generations.

## Inputs
`CAP-01`, `FND-03`, metadata policy `GATE-11`, and source-class rules in `D8`.

## Outputs
closed-file streaming; pre/post metadata observation; unique staged copies; bounded retry for changing files; before/after tree enumeration; admitted symlink and metadata capture; stable-tree evidence; last-exact-acquisition retention.

## Declared prerequisites
`CAP-01`, `FND-03`, `GATE-11`.

## Traceability
`R1`, `R4`, `R5`, `R7`, `R11`, `D8`, `D10`, `S04`, `Q06`, `RC02`, `RC04`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `CAP-03`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
every attempt has a distinct staging identity; all acquired exact versions may be retained as evidence, while only validated candidates reach the coordinator. Producer files are never locked destructively or rewritten.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
a stable file/tree can be classified complete under its declared strategy; a changing JSONL or tree remains `open`, `unstable`, or `partial`; no byte splice, last-line repair, or parser success is used as capture proof.

## Required tests or review evidence
concurrent append, truncate, replace, rename, directory addition/removal, symlink swap, non-UTF-8 path, missing sidecar, metadata change, bounded-retry exhaustion, and stable rerun over synthetic trees.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
implement the accepted per-filesystem WAL capture method and a separately classified logical read-snapshot path.

## Inputs
`CAP-01`, `FND-03`, accepted `GATE-02`, SQLite source-class contract, and exact/logical artifact distinction.

## Outputs
DB/WAL/SHM bundle membership and coherence evidence; approved filesystem/quiescence helper where available; Online Backup, `VACUUM INTO`, or `sqlite3_rsync` logical snapshots as selected; separate hashes, classes, provenance, and diagnostics.

## Declared prerequisites
`CAP-01`, `FND-03`, `GATE-02`.

## Traceability
`R1`, `R5`, `R8`, `R11`, `D8`, `D10`, `D16`, `E03`, `Q05`, `S05`, `RC03`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `CAP-04`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
producer databases open read-only; no checkpoint, vacuum, migration, repair, or reindex runs against them. Failure leaves source files unchanged and prior complete archive generations active.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
an exact bundle is complete only under the method proved by `GATE-02`; a logical database image is never labeled as the producer’s exact artifact set; uncertain bundles remain retained but `incoherent` or `unknown`.

## Required tests or review evidence
the accepted concurrent-WAL matrix, missing/changing WAL or SHM, journal-mode change, failed logical SQLite backup, busy/locked database, snapshot interruption, integrity check, transaction visibility, artifact-class confusion, and unknown schema.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
compose inventory, reachability, acquisition, object insertion, manifest commitment, derived publication, indexing, verification, and status into one idempotent run.

## Inputs
`GATE-04`, `AUTH-01`–`AUTH-03`, `STATE-01`, and `CAP-01`–`CAP-04`.

## Outputs
role-aware coordinator commands; bounded host/source/hash/reader concurrency; all-cell inventory; prioritized hook-hint queue; deterministic per-source commit serialization; fourteen-step run state machine; index handoff; final receipt and exit status.

## Declared prerequisites
`GATE-04`, `AUTH-01`, `AUTH-02`, `AUTH-03`, `STATE-01`, `CAP-01`, `CAP-02`, `CAP-03`, `CAP-04`.

## Traceability
`R3`, `R4`, `R5`, `R7`, `R8`, `R10`, `R12`, `D7`, `D9`, `D10`, `D15`, `S01`, `S02`, `S03`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `ORCH-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
one lock protects authority. New acquisition stops before disk exhaustion; committed authority is never rolled back; mutable heads, derived pointers, and retries reconstruct from manifests.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
an unchanged source transfers or inserts no verified payload and creates no redundant manifest; one source failure does not block unrelated sources; every enrolled cell receives a state; crashes at each boundary converge safely on rerun; no derived failure rolls back authority.

## Required tests or review evidence
model/state-machine and integration tests for all fourteen steps, all crash points, duplicate runs, concurrent sources, per-source serialization, offline hosts, low-space stop, unknown fingerprint, changing capture, manifest commit, and derived/index failure.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
preserve Pi versioned tree-shaped JSONL without flattening branch-in-place semantics.

## Inputs
`CAP-01`, `CAP-02`, `CAP-03`, installed Pi format specification, and `GATE-11`.

## Outputs
session-root discovery, header/version fingerprint, whole-file generation capture, custom/unknown-record support, and source-state classification.

** Design

the adapter never invokes `/share`, rewrites a session, or treats a reader-selected branch as the archived whole.

Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

## Required tests or review evidence
synthetic version 3 trees, in-file branches, compaction, branch summaries, forks, labels, custom/unknown records, append during copy, malformed record, no session root, and relocated session directory.

## Purpose
preserve the current WAL-mode OpenCode store and legacy JSON while keeping exact native bundles separate from logical reader snapshots.

## Inputs
`CAP-01`, `CAP-02`, `CAP-03`, `CAP-04`, OpenCode findings, and accepted `GATE-02`.

## Outputs
current/legacy root discovery, SQLite journal/schema fingerprint, exact bundle plan, logical snapshot plan, migration/empty-store state, and explicit schema-retention uncertainty.

## Declared prerequisites
`CAP-01`, `CAP-02`, `CAP-03`, `CAP-04`, `GATE-02`.

## Traceability
`R1`, `R3`, `R5`, `R7`, `R11`, `D8`, `D10`, `S05`, `RC03`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `SRC-04`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
no producer checkpoint, vacuum, migration, or reindex occurs. An unproved exact bundle remains incomplete without blocking preservation.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
database, matching WAL/SHM, and legacy artifacts retain class/provenance; a logical SQLite backup supports readers but cannot substitute for exact artifacts; schema migration preserves prior generations.

## Required tests or review evidence
synthetic WAL writes, schema migration, missing/rotating WAL, legacy JSON fallback, empty directory, user deletion, unknown table/column, incoherent bundle, and logical-snapshot rebuild.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
expose selected manifest generations in original relative layouts without allowing readers to mutate authoritative objects.

## Inputs
`AUTH-03`, `FND-03`, and hierarchy `D5`.

## Outputs
generic per-host/source/manifest native-tree materialization; verified read-only copy or copy-on-write reflink policy; atomic current-generation pointer.

## Declared prerequisites
`AUTH-03`, `FND-03`.

## Traceability
`R1`, `R2`, `R6`, `D5`, `D8`, `D11`, `S09`, `RC06`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `VIEW-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
write a fresh derived generation, verify it, then switch one disposable pointer. Failure preserves both authority and the prior current view.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
every output byte verifies against its object; readers have no writable alias to `native/`; identical bytes may be reused safely without losing occurrence paths; rebuild from manifests produces the same selected layout.

## Required tests or review evidence
synthetic layouts for regular files, directories, symlinks, admitted metadata, non-UTF-8 paths, and duplicate occurrences; permissions; attempted mutation; reflink unsupported fallback; atomic switch failure; concurrent reader; complete deletion/rebuild.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
persist reader projections, native locators, lineage claims, facets, text, and coverage in a disposable, versioned SQLite/FTS generation.

## Inputs
accepted `GATE-05`; `RDR-01`; format readers as available; index contract `D11`.

## Outputs
index schema/version; manifest and reader watermarks; source/session/relationship/projection/coverage tables; FTS; transactional incremental update; fresh-generation rebuild; integrity/coverage validation; atomic active pointer.

## Declared prerequisites
`GATE-05`, `RDR-01`.

## Traceability
`R2`, `R6`, `R9`, `R10`, `R11`, `D11`, `D17`, `S01`, `S06`, `S09`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `IDX-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
build a new generation or commit one transaction, validate it, then switch the disposable pointer. A failed update leaves the prior index active and never changes authority.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
every indexed row includes an exact manifest/native locator and reader/fingerprint version; older generations remain queryable or rebuildable; unsupported/partial/opaque counts are visible; deleting the database permits full reconstruction.

## Required tests or review evidence
synthetic projections from every reader contract; duplicate/collision/branch lineage; transaction interruption; index corruption; old/new schema; incremental versus full-build equivalence; FTS query; coverage reconciliation; complete deletion/rebuild.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
provide local lexical search, filtering, coverage, native retrieval, interpreted display, and explicit lossy-export actions without a network service.

## Inputs
`IDX-01`, `VIEW-01`, accepted `GATE-08`, and query semantics `D11`.

## Outputs
local CLI commands for search/facets/history/coverage, open/stream native, show interpreted, source status, and explicit export selection; escaped snippets and control characters; exact object/manifest locators.

## Declared prerequisites
`IDX-01`, `VIEW-01`, `GATE-08`.

## Traceability
`R8`, `R9`, `R12`, `D11`, `D14`, `D15`, `E07`, `Q09`, `Q10`, `Q14`.

## Governing source

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
CLI reads owner-only local state. Query and display changes require no authority migration; disabling an optional viewer leaves baseline search available.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
default search selects current complete or last-complete-offline generations and visibly reports excluded coverage; native and interpreted views are distinct; a search hit resolves to exact object bytes; no remote listener exists.

## Required tests or review evidence
all facets, historical/unstable opt-in, partial/opaque coverage, offline source age, locator round-trip, malicious HTML/terminal text, binary attachment, no-result under incomplete coverage, and native byte verification.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
run explicitly selected ATIF, Letta, OpenTelemetry/OpenInference, HTML, DuckDB, or other exporters/readers without making their runtimes or schemas core dependencies.

## Inputs
`FND-03`, `RDR-01`, `VIEW-01`, `GATE-06`, and plugin boundary `D8`/`D12`.

## Outputs
versioned stdin/stdout/file protocol; input-manifest/reader/tool/options provenance; fresh derived-output directory; network-off execution; read-only selected inputs; timeout/resource/error handling; loss/omission diagnostics.

## Declared prerequisites
`FND-03`, `RDR-01`, `VIEW-01`, `GATE-06`.

## Traceability
`R2`, `R8`, `R11`, `D8`, `D12`, `D14`, `RC01`, `RC06`, `RC07`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `PLUG-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
plugins receive no authority write path, SSH credential, ambient network, or shared output generation. Removing all plugin outputs leaves baseline operation intact.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
plugin failure cannot affect collection, authority verification, baseline index/search, or recovery; Python/TypeScript closures are optional; external destination/upload requires a separate explicit action and confirmation.

## Required tests or review evidence
synthetic successful/lossy/partial export, malformed plugin output, timeout, crash, network attempt, authority write attempt, secret environment access, output-path escape, version change, and deletion/rebuild.

## Purpose
define source-linked query projections, diagnostics, coverage, and version/fingerprint support independently of any native source format.

## Inputs
`AUTH-02`, `AUTH-03`, `CAP-01`, accepted `GATE-11`, and semantic-reader contract `D8`; `GATE-06` determines reuse within format-specific readers.

## Outputs
versioned reader interface; exact source locator; namespaced native IDs and confidence-labeled relationships; timestamp/facet/text/token projections; omitted/unknown counts; `unsupported-fingerprint`, `partial-parse`, `malformed-record`, and `missing-sidecar` diagnostics.

## Declared prerequisites
`AUTH-02`, `AUTH-03`, `CAP-01`, `GATE-11`.

## Traceability
`R2`, `R6`, `R7`, `R8`, `R9`, `R11`, `D8`, `D10`, `D11`, `D17`, `S06`, `S08`, `RC06`, `RC10`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `RDR-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
readers receive read-only authority/views and write only new derived output. A prior reader/index generation remains selectable until replacement coverage passes.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
an unsupported or malformed record affects only its projection and coverage; unknown fields remain retrievable by native locator; reader upgrades never rewrite authority; repeated text is not content-deduplicated.

## Required tests or review evidence
contract/property tests for complete, partial, malformed, opaque, unknown-version, duplicate, collision, parent/branch, missing-sidecar, time-confidence, and locator round-trip cases; secret and active-content escaping.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Outputs
versioned schema/table/column support; session/message/part/project relationships; native locators into the logical snapshot and exact bundle provenance; legacy reader; opaque-field coverage.

## Purpose
expose actionable source/run/index/authority health without leaking transcript content, native paths, command output, or credentials, and without asserting external-backup health.

## Inputs
`STATE-01`, `ORCH-01`, `IDX-01`, accepted `GATE-08`, and operations contract `D15`.

## Outputs
structured local logs, exit codes, status model, counters/gauges/histograms, healthy/degraded/unhealthy evaluation, alerts, privileged local diagnostics, bounded concurrency controls, and free-space safety floor.

## Declared prerequisites
`STATE-01`, `ORCH-01`, `IDX-01`, `GATE-08`.

## Traceability
`R3`, `R8`, `R12`, `R15`, `D15`, `S02`, `Q09`, `RC10`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `OBS-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
observability failure cannot alter authority or block verification/search. Optional OpenTelemetry remains disabled and content-free; prior local status remains sufficient.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
status covers every enrolled cell and reports last attempt/success/complete age, state, counts, object reuse, manifest/index watermarks, retries, and error class; low-space stops acquisition before authority corruption; no content field reaches ordinary output; no field claims an external backup exists or is healthy.

## Required tests or review evidence
state matrix, offline/partial/unknown source, index lag, hash/chain failure, low disk, growth anomaly, concurrency limits, diagnostic privilege, secret/path canaries, absence of backup-health fields, and optional content-free telemetry export.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
evolve manifests, object-hash namespaces, source fingerprints, readers, indexes, source enrollment, and vendor exports without rewriting prior authority.

## Inputs
`AUTH-02`, `RDR-01`, `IDX-01`, version policy `D17`.

## Outputs
manifest decoder/version dispatch; additive manifest writer versions; parallel hash namespace/cross-hash model; fingerprint support declarations; reader/index invalidation rules; source migration record; export source-kind versioning; rollback selection.

## Declared prerequisites
`AUTH-02`, `RDR-01`, `IDX-01`.

## Traceability
`R6`, `R7`, `R10`, `R11`, `D10`, `D17`, `S06`, `S08`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `MIG-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
migrations create new authority only where the design calls for successor manifests; they never edit old objects/manifests. Derived rollback selects the prior generation.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
old manifests remain byte-identical and readable; a new unknown source fingerprint still preserves; reader/index upgrades rebuild only derived data; source move/rename retains both occurrences and explicit continuity claims.

## Required tests or review evidence
old/new manifest versions, unknown future fields, parallel hash namespace, reader upgrade/downgrade, index rollback, source host/path/account migration, duplicate native IDs, export version, and interrupted migration.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
back up the complete authority scope to failure-independent encrypted repositories and prove that snapshots are usable.

## Inputs
`AUTH-03`, `ORCH-01`, accepted `GATE-03`, `GATE-07`, `GATE-09`, and backup contract `D16`.

## Outputs
fixed restic include/exclude scope for `VERSION`, `config/`, `native/`, and `manifests/`; credential-command integration; repository snapshot receipts; covered manifest heads; retention/prune guard; integrity check; sampled/full restore commands and receipts.

## Declared prerequisites
`AUTH-03`, `ORCH-01`, `GATE-03`, `GATE-07`, `GATE-09`.

## Traceability
`R1`, `R8`, `R10`, `R15`, `D5`, `D9`, `D14`, `D16`, `E01`, `E06`, `E09`, `Q08`, `S01`, `S10`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `BACK-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
restic never becomes the browse namespace or discovery source. Backup failure leaves local authority untouched; prune is disabled until retention proof passes.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
every due repository records a snapshot ID and coverage after authority verification; one failure is visible without invalidating another; prune cannot remove the sole retained copy; sampled restore verifies manifest/object hashes in a fresh root.

## Required tests or review evidence
synthetic first/incremental backup, deduplication, one/two repositories, wrong/lost credentials, interrupted backup, repository corruption/check, retention prune, held generation, sampled/full restore, and receipt leakage scan.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

* DEFER [#C] REC-01 — Implement clean recovery from native authority    :rec:
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       sess-ek0.5.4
:LABELS:   ["expanded-scope","implementation-plan","phase-4","rec","session-archive","work-package"]
:ISSUE_TYPE: task

:MODIFIED: [2026-08-13 Thu 13:45]
:CREATED_BY: pi

:EXTERNAL_REF: implementation-plan:REC-01
:SOURCE_REPO: sessions
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/sessions
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

## Purpose
accept authority already restored or otherwise supplied by the operator in a clean root and reconstruct an operational archive without source hosts, mutable state, materialized trees, or an old index.

## Inputs
`AUTH-03`, `STATE-01`, `VIEW-01`, `IDX-01`, operator-supplied `VERSION`, versioned `config/` including `readers.lock`, `native/`, `manifests/`, and recovery sequence `D16`.

## Outputs
supplied-root ownership/permission and compatibility checks; authority verification; chain/head reconstruction; native-view materialization; logical read-snapshot recreation where valid; fresh index build; manifest-derived total comparison; native-open/search smoke checks; safe source re-enrollment boundary; explicitly labeled salvage mode.

## Declared prerequisites
`AUTH-03`, `STATE-01`, `VIEW-01`, `IDX-01`.

## Traceability
`R1`, `R2`, `R6`, `R9`, `R10`, `R11`, `D4`, `D16`, `S09`, `S10`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `REC-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
recovery writes a new private root or validates a designated isolated root and never repairs from derived output. Failed authority or configuration verification prevents operational activation and leaves the operator-supplied input unchanged; salvage mode is read-only and cannot resume collection.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
full R10 recovery validates operator-supplied `VERSION`, `config/`, `native/`, and `manifests/`; exact objects and provenance verify; state and search rebuild from scratch under pinned readers; unsupported fingerprints leave bytes intact and coverage explicit; recovered authority is healthy before source reconnection. If config is missing, only raw-byte/manifest-provenance salvage is available: policy, trusted interpretation, search claims, and re-enrollment remain disabled until config is supplied or explicitly reconstructed and reviewed. No outcome asserts external-backup existence or health.

## Required tests or review evidence
clean-machine synthetic recovery from a pre-populated authority directory with no state/derived/source hosts; unsafe ownership/permissions; missing-config salvage mode; wrong/incompatible config or readers lock; unsupported fingerprint; missing/corrupt object; chain break; unavailable logical SQLite recreation; index rebuild; manifest-total mismatch; and first successor after re-enrollment.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0

## Purpose
provide pinned, reproducible package/app/development/check outputs for Darwin arm64, Linux arm64, and Linux x86_64.

## Inputs
`FND-01`, accepted `GATE-01`, and package outputs in `D13`.

## Outputs
`packages.<system>.session-archive`, default package, app, development shell, unit/integration/package checks, and pinned declarations for OpenSSH, rsync, SQLite, and optional plugin closures.

## Declared prerequisites
`FND-01`, `GATE-01`.

## Traceability
`R13`, `R14`, `D12`, `D13`, `E05`, `Q13`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `NIX-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
packaging creates store closures only; it does not create users, directories, services, credentials, archive state, or network access.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
every target evaluates, builds, runs CLI smoke tests, and executes unit/integration/package checks in its pinned closure; optional plugins do not enlarge the baseline closure; runtime tool versions are inspectable.

## Required tests or review evidence
clean three-system build/evaluation, closure inspection, missing external tool, CLI help/version, test suite, shell tooling, optional plugin disabled/enabled, and no secret material in the Nix store.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.
