* DEFER [#C] NIX-02 — Implement source and hub NixOS/nix-darwin role modules    :nix:
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       sess-ek0.5.6
:LABELS:   ["expanded-scope","implementation-plan","nix","phase-4","session-archive","work-package"]
:ISSUE_TYPE: task
:CREATED:  [2026-08-12 Wed 15:04]
:MODIFIED: [2026-08-13 Thu 13:45]
:CREATED_BY: pi
:DEFERRED: [2126-01-01 Tue 09:00]
:EXTERNAL_REF: implementation-plan:NIX-02
:SOURCE_REPO: sessions
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/sessions
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

## Purpose
express least-privilege source/helper and hub/coordinator roles without producing a second writer or embedding secrets in Nix.

## Inputs
`FND-02`, `FND-03`, `ORCH-01`, `OBS-01`, `NIX-01`, and accepted `GATE-03`, `GATE-04`, `GATE-07`, `GATE-08`.

## Outputs
NixOS source/hub modules; nix-darwin source/hub modules; options for IDs, labels, allowlisted roots, schedules, concurrency, store path, source-access credential references, and feature flags; systemd/launchd units and timers disabled until configured. No backup option, credential hook, job, timer, or status field is exposed.

## Declared prerequisites
`FND-02`, `FND-03`, `ORCH-01`, `OBS-01`, `NIX-01`, `GATE-03`, `GATE-04`, `GATE-07`, `GATE-08`.

## Traceability
`R8`, `R12`, `R13`, `R15`, `D7`, `D12`, `D13`, `D14`, `D15`, `D16`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `NIX-02`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
module tests do not deploy. Later rollback disables units/timers first, preserves authority, and removes only package/runtime state after verification.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
module evaluation rejects world-readable paths, secret values, invalid roots, hub/source writer collision, unsafe helper commands, and any attempted backup configuration; source hosts install no resident network service; hub role has one coordinator; generated units use owner-only runtime paths and source-access credential helpers.

## Required tests or review evidence
module evaluation for all roles/systems; option defaults and rejection cases; unit hardening; schedule disabled/enabled; source-access credential reference; rejection of backup options; source helper forced command; duplicate writer; package closure; service start/stop/restart on synthetic roots.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
validate the integrated system against every scenario in design section 19 and the complete requirement set.

## Inputs
all baseline production packages through `NIX-02`; accepted gates; scenario ledger `S01`–`S10`; `PLUG-01` evidence only when that optional package is enabled.

## Outputs
reproducible end-to-end harness and evidence bundle for normal/unchanged collection, offline catch-up, crash boundaries, live JSONL, live SQLite, identifier collision, resume/fork/branch, schema drift, index loss/rebuild, and hub loss/recovery.

## Declared prerequisites
`SRC-01`, `SRC-02`, `SRC-03`, `SRC-04`, `SRC-05`, `SRC-06`, `SRC-07`, `SRC-08`, `SRC-09`, `RDR-02`, `RDR-03`, `RDR-04`, `RDR-05`, `RDR-06`, `RDR-07`, `RDR-08`, `RDR-09`, `RDR-10`, `IDX-02`, `OBS-01`, `MIG-01`, `REC-01`, `NIX-02`.

## Traceability
`R1`–`R15`, `D4`–`D18`, `S01`–`S10`, `RC01`–`RC10`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `E2E-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
all data and services are synthetic and isolated; no external backup endpoint, repository, or credential is used. A failed scenario blocks release readiness and cannot be waived by unrelated green tests.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
each scenario starts from a verified manifest head, executes the documented failure/transition, and asserts the exact R1–R15 outcomes; deleting all state/derived products and rebuilding from an operator-supplied authority directory do not impair native recovery; unknown desktop/reader coverage remains explicit; optional plugin results are tested when enabled and otherwise recorded deferred.

## Required tests or review evidence
the ten scenarios are the tests; each runs under the relevant target-system closure, with crash/fault injection and secret/path canaries where applicable.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

* DEFER [#C] DOC-01 — Write operator, recovery, privacy, and extension documentation    :doc:
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       sess-ek0.6.2
:LABELS:   ["doc","expanded-scope","implementation-plan","phase-5","session-archive","work-package"]
:ISSUE_TYPE: task
:CREATED:  [2026-08-12 Wed 15:04]
:MODIFIED: [2026-08-13 Thu 13:45]
:CREATED_BY: pi
:DEFERRED: [2126-01-01 Tue 09:00]
:EXTERNAL_REF: implementation-plan:DOC-01
:SOURCE_REPO: sessions
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /Users/johnw/src/sessions
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

## Purpose
document the verified commands, invariants, status meanings, recovery procedure, privacy controls, adapter/reader extension rules, and deployment authorization boundary.

## Inputs
`E2E-01`, `GATE-08`, `GATE-09`, all accepted decisions, and actual CLI/module behavior.

## Outputs
local install/build/check guide; configuration/enrollment reference; source-state and coverage glossary; collection/search/verify/recover runbook; operator-managed backup boundary; incident and low-space procedure; adapter/reader/plugin extension guide; privacy/fixture policy; migration policy; deployment and rollback checklist.

## Declared prerequisites
`E2E-01`, `GATE-08`, `GATE-09`.

## Traceability
`R1`–`R15`, `D4`–`D18`, `Q01`–`Q15`, `S01`–`S10`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `DOC-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
documentation never instructs remote mutation or private ingestion without an explicit approval checkpoint. Destructive operations are absent unless `GATE-09` accepted them.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
every documented command is exercised by a check or walkthrough; authority versus derived state is unambiguous; unknown states and destructive prohibitions are prominent; external backup is accurately documented as operator-managed and unobserved by the tool; no credentials, private paths, transcript excerpts, invented SLOs, or unsupported claims appear.

## Required tests or review evidence
command/link validation, clean-reader walkthrough, recovery rehearsal against the runbook, sensitive-content scan, stale-version check, and cross-reference audit to R1–R15 and S01–S10.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

:PROPERTIES:

## Purpose
decide whether the locally verified implementation is fit to enter a separately authorized deployment goal.

## Inputs
all gate decisions, `E2E-01`, `DOC-01`, package/test evidence, and unresolved-question ledger.

## Outputs
requirement/scenario/evidence matrix; accepted/deferred/failed gate register; residual-risk register; deployment prerequisites; explicit no-go conditions; and applicability criteria for the future implementation issue tree.

## Declared prerequisites
`DOC-01`, `E2E-01`, `GATE-01`, `GATE-02`, `GATE-03`, `GATE-04`, `GATE-05`, `GATE-06`, `GATE-07`, `GATE-08`, `GATE-09`, `GATE-10`, `GATE-11`.

## Traceability
`R1`–`R15`, `D4`–`D18`, `E01`–`E09`, `Q01`–`Q15`, `RC01`–`RC10`, `S01`–`S10`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `REL-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
readiness is documentary and revocable. It does not create issues, deploy, ingest, connect to fleet hosts, or satisfy `APR-01`.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
every `R`, `D`, `E`, `Q`, `RC`, and `S` locator maps to accepted evidence, an explicit retained unknown, or a blocking gate; no incomplete authority, recovery, security, low-space, or required source condition is labeled locally ready; no external-backup claim is made.

## Required tests or review evidence
programmatic evidence-coverage validation; full check suite on all target systems; diff/license/secret/privacy review; independent review of authority, recovery, and rollout boundaries.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       sess-ek0.7

## Purpose
Specify a future, separately authorized rollout without blocking or satisfying the local first-version root.

## Packages
`APR-01`–`APR-08` and `DEP-01`–`DEP-08`

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, sections 4.1 and 11.

## Authorization boundary
This epic is detached from the local root. Every mutating stage remains serially blocked by its own explicit manual approval package. External backup remains operator-managed throughout and is never accessed, configured, tested, or reported by the tool.

## Success Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure is considered only under a later confirmed rollout goal and is not a condition of local release.

** Acceptance Criteria

- All 16 rollout package issues close under their individual contracts only after their exact stage approvals.
- Every approval package records an explicit user decision; no approval carries forward implicitly.
- Every mutating stage verifies authority, recovery behavior, health, and rollback before the next stage.
- No stage accesses, configures, tests, or asserts the health of an external backup.
- This epic remains detached from, and is not required for, the local first-version root.

:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17

:END:

## Purpose
obtain explicit user authorization for a deployment-preparation goal limited to synthetic rehearsal.

## Inputs
`REL-01` readiness record, residual risks, proposed host/role matrix, source-access credentials plan, rollback boundaries, and exact proposed preparation actions.

## Outputs
recorded user decision naming the allowed synthetic/local scope, prohibited private-data and remote-mutation boundaries, stop conditions, and approval provenance.

## Declared prerequisites
`REL-01`, `GATE-03`, `GATE-07`, `GATE-08`.

## Traceability
`R8`, `R10`, `R12`, `R13`, `R15`, `D7`, `D13`–`D16`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `APR-01`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
this package performs no technical action. It remains open until explicit approval and may be revoked before `DEP-01`.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
the user explicitly approves the stated preparation scope in the approval issue or linked goal confirmation; silence, prior architectural approval, and issue readiness do not qualify.

## Required tests or review evidence
review the proposed scope against `REL-01`; confirm that it permits no private source enrollment, remote mutation, or unattended capture.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
mutate and enroll only the host authorized by `APR-05`, then verify collection, authority, recovery behavior, and rollback before another host is considered.

## Inputs
accepted `APR-05`.

## Outputs
host mutation receipt, per-cell/source states, first/unchanged/offline-catch-up runs, authority health, recovery evidence, and rollback evidence.

## Declared prerequisites
`APR-05`.

## Traceability
`R1`–`R15`, `D7`–`D18`, `S01`–`S10`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `DEP-05`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
pause before another host. Disable the host helper/collection while retaining verified hub authority.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
the approved host verifies all applicable source and operational contracts; unapproved hosts remain untouched.

## Required tests or review evidence
package/helper lifecycle, pinned SSH pull, source canaries, offline catch-up, live-store rules, low-space stop, supplied-authority recovery, and host rollback.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
add the approved hub-local sources one source class at a time before any remote mutation.

## Inputs
accepted `APR-04`.

## Outputs
per-source enrollment receipts, first/unchanged runs, coverage, authority checks, alerts, and rollback evidence.

## Declared prerequisites
`APR-04`.

## Traceability
`R1`–`R15`, `D7`–`D18`, `S01`, `S03`–`S10`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `DEP-04`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
pause on any failed gate, unexplained growth, incomplete snapshot, privacy finding, or recovery defect. Disable only the failing source while retaining verified authority.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
every approved hub-local source verifies authority, coverage, capacity, and rollback before the next begins; remote hosts remain untouched.

## Required tests or review evidence
staged local enrollment, live JSONL/SQLite rules, unknown/absent states, low-space stop, authority failure, supplied-authority recovery, and per-source rollback.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

## Purpose
obtain explicit user authorization for exactly one not-yet-enrolled remote host and select it as stage 1.

## Inputs
accepted `DEP-04`, the four remaining host candidates (`clio`, `vps`, `vulcan`, `andoria-08`), exact proposed host mutation/helper scope, source roots, source-access credentials, capacity/authority health, and rollback commands.

## Outputs
recorded user decision naming one host, its role, allowed mutations and roots, stage-specific stop conditions, and the still-blocked remaining hosts.

## Declared prerequisites
`DEP-04`.

## Traceability
`R3`, `R8`, `R10`, `R12`, `R13`, `R15`, `D7`, `D13`–`D16`.

## Governing source
`doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`, package `APR-05`. The package ID and approved contract are stable; implementation details may be refined only without weakening this contract.

## Acceptance Criteria
See the dedicated acceptance-criteria field. Closure requires both its completion evidence and its required tests or review evidence.

** Design

## Safety and rollback
this package performs no connection or mutation. Any target or scope change requires renewed approval.

## Contract boundary
Preserve the architecture, authority, privacy, and authorization boundaries in `doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md`. A blocked or failed gate remains open; do not substitute proxy evidence or silently broaden this package.

** Acceptance Criteria

## Completion evidence
the user explicitly approves one named host in this approval issue; no other remote host or mutation is implied.

## Required tests or review evidence
review host identity, pinned SSH key, module/helper diff, roots/exclusions, source gates, capacity, authority health, and rollback against the approval.

Do not close this issue until both sections are satisfied with concrete evidence recorded in the issue or linked artifacts.

* DONE [#C] Preserve sparse session files during gathering
:PROPERTIES:
:OBR_SCHEMA_VERSION: 17
:ID:       sess-i1o
:LABELS:   []
:ISSUE_TYPE: bug
:CREATED:  [2026-08-14 Fri 11:23]
:MODIFIED: [2026-08-14 Fri 11:24]
:CREATED_BY: johnw
:FINISHED: [2026-08-14 Fri 11:24]
:SOURCE_REPO: sessions
:SOURCE_REPO_PATH: /home/johnw/src/sessions
:ORIGINAL_SIZE: 0
:END:

`bin/gather-sessions` invoked `rsync -a --checksum --protect-args`, with no
`--sparse`. Several agent session files on the Andoria NFS source are very large
but mostly holes -- Codex measured about 157 GiB allocated against 399 GiB
apparent -- so a non-sparse copy writes those holes out in full. The existing
Hera archive expanded them: about 470 GiB allocated for a tree that should need
roughly 220 GiB.

Add `--sparse` to the single rsync invocation so holes survive the copy, and
prove it stays added.

** Close Reason

#+begin_example
Added --sparse to the single rsync invocation in bin/gather-sessions.

Transfer is now equivalent to:
    rsync -a --sparse --checksum --protect-args -- SOURCE/ DESTINATION/
No --inplace, --delete, or --remove-source-files was introduced.

Verified, not just asserted:
  - exact-argv test updated. The fake rsync captured six positional arguments;
    --sparse makes seven and shifts SOURCE from $5 to $6, so both the capture
    and the badhost check were corrected alongside the expectation.
  - new sparse regression: builds a 64 MiB fixture holding one short line of
    real data, gathers it, and asserts (a) source and copy compare byte-identical,
    (b) the copy's apparent size is still 64 MiB, and (c) its allocated blocks
    stay under half that and within 4x the source's allocation.
  - the allocation assertion is guarded on the SOURCE fixture actually being
    sparse. If the filesystem does not store holes the fixture comes back fully
    allocated, and asserting on the copy would then fail for a reason unrelated
    to the gatherer; in that case the test prints a skip line instead. Confirmed
    on this host that the assertion RAN rather than skipping.
  - regression-detection proven: with --sparse removed the suite exits 1, with it
    restored the suite exits 0. A test that passes either way would be worthless.
  - shellcheck clean, shfmt -d -i 2 -ci clean, bash -n clean, full suite passes.

Documentation updated so no stale invocation remains anywhere: doc/DESIGN.md
(required behavior plus a new sparse bullet), doc/IMPLEMENTATION.md (both local
and SSH forms), README.md (requirements now name --protect-args, --checksum and
--sparse; test section describes the sparse case and its skip condition).
Verified by grep that no 'rsync -a --checksum' invocation survives in bin, test,
README or doc.
#+end_example
